OTHER FINANCIAL INFORMATION                                                         其他財務資料
Non-IFRS Financial Measures                                                         非國際財務報告準則財務計量
To supplement our consolidated results, which are presented in accordance           為補充根據國際財務報告準則呈列的綜合業
with IFRS, we also use certain non-IFRS measures, namely adjusted EBITDA            績，我們亦使用若干非國際財務報告準則計量
and adjusted net profit for the period, as additional financial metrics. These      （即經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利淨額）作為
non-IFRS financial measures are not required by or presented in accordance          額外財務指標。該等非國際財務報告準則財務
with IFRS.                                                                          計量並非國際財務報告準則所規定，亦並非按
                                                                                    其呈列。


Adjusted EBITDA is defined as operating profit adjusted for (i) share-based         經調整EBITDA定義為經以下項目調整的經營溢
compensation, gross; (ii) amortization of intangible assets; (iii) depreciation     利：(i)以股份為基礎的酬金總額；(ii)無形資產攤
of property, plant and equipment, and right-of-use assets; (iv) foreign             銷；(iii)物業、廠房及設備和使用權資產折舊；
exchange loss/(gain), net; (v) net gains on investees; and (vi) others,             (iv)匯兌虧損 ╱（收益）淨額；(v)被投資方的收益
including acquisition-related cost and net gains on foreign currency financial      淨額；及(vi)其他，包括收購相關成本及外幣金
instruments. Adjusted net profit for the period is defined as profit for the        融工具的淨收益。期內經調整溢利淨額定義為
period adjusted for (i) share-based compensation, net; (ii) the amortization of     經以下項目調整的期內溢利：(i) 以股份為基礎
intangible assets from acquisition; (iii) foreign exchange loss/(gain), net; (iv)   的酬金淨額；(ii)收購產生的無形資產攤銷；(iii)
net gains on investees; and (v) others, including acquisition-related cost, net     匯兌虧損 ╱（收益）淨額；(iv)被投資方的收益淨
gains on foreign currency financial instruments and depreciation of property,       額；及(v)其他，包括收購相關成本、外幣金融
plant and equipment and right-of-use assets from acquisition.                       工具的淨收益以及已收購物業、廠房及設備和
                                                                                    使用權資產的折舊。


The above items are excluded from our adjusted EBITDA and adjusted net              上述項目從經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利
profit for the period measures because these items are either non-cash in           淨額的計量中剔除乃由於該等項目屬於非現金
nature, or are not driven by core operations which render comparisons with          性質，或並非受核心業務所驅動，導致其與過
prior periods and competitors less meaningful. We believe the adjusted              往期間及競爭對手的比較意義不大。我們認為
EBITDA and adjusted net profit for the period are useful measures for the           經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利淨額對分析師
analysts and investors as basis for evaluation of our future on-going operating     和投資者而言屬有用的計量指標，可作為評估
performance as these measures allow more meaningful comparison of our               我們未來持續經營表現的依據，原因是該等計
performance and projected cash earnings with our historical results from            量指標可讓我們的表現及預測現金收益與我們
prior periods and to the results of our competitors. Moreover, management           過往期間的歷史業績及競爭對手的業績進行更
uses these measures internally to evaluate the performance of our business          具意義的比較。此外，管理層內部使用該等計
as a whole. However, our presentation of such non-IFRS measures may not             量指標來評估我們的整體業務表現。然而，該
be comparable to similarly titled measures presented by other companies.            等非國際財務報告準則計量的呈列未必可與其
The use of these non-IFRS measures has limitations as an analytical tool, and       他公司所列類似計量指標相比。該等非國際財
you should not consider it in isolation from, or as substitute for analysis of,     務報告準則計量作為分析工具的使用存在局限
our results of operations or financial condition as reported under IFRS.            性，不應視其為獨立於或可代替我們根據國際
                                                                                    財務報告準則所呈報經營業績或財務狀況的分

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                            外匯風險
Foreign exchange risk arises when future commercial transactions or              當未來的商業交易或已確認的資產及負債不是
recognized assets and liabilities are denominated in a currency that is not      以我們的功能貨幣計價時，即產生外匯風險。
our functional currency. We manage our foreign exchange risk by performing       我們通過定期審查我們的外匯淨風險敞口管理
regular reviews of our net foreign exchange exposures.                           外匯風險。


Our subsidiaries and Consolidated Affiliated Entities in the PRC operate         我們的中國附屬公司及併表聯屬實體主要在中
mainly in the PRC with most of the transactions settled in RMB. There are        國境內經營，而大部分交易以人民幣結算。
certain financial assets or liabilities of our Group which are denominated in    本集團有若干金融資產或負債以本集團於中國
the currencies other than the respective functional currencies of our Group’s    營運的附屬公司及併表聯屬實體各自功能貨幣
subsidiaries and Consolidated Affiliated Entities operating in the PRC, and      以外的貨幣計值，而截至二零二六年六月三十
as of June 30, 2026, the Group entered into certain derivative contracts with    日，本集團與金融機構訂立若干用作經濟對沖
financial institutions which are used for economic hedging purposes. These       用途的衍生合約。該等衍生合約列作衍生金融
derivative contracts are accounted for as derivative financial instruments and   工具並以公允價值計量。
measured at fair value.

     The purpose of the 2018 Share Incentive Plan is to promote the success             二零一八年股份激勵計劃旨在通過將本公司及
     and enhance the value of our Company, by linking the personal interests of         本公司任何現時或未來附屬公司或VIE（各定義見
     the directors, officers and employees of our Company and any present or            下文）董事、高級職員及僱員的個人利益掛鈎，
     future subsidiaries or VIEs (each term as defined below) of our Company by         藉著激勵該等人士作出傑出表現，為股東帶來
     providing such individuals with an incentive for outstanding performance,          豐厚回報，以促進本公司的成功及提升其價
     to generate superior returns to the Shareholders. The 2018 Share Incentive         值。二零一八年股份激勵計劃亦旨在使本公司
     Plan is further intended to provide flexibility to our Company in its ability to   能靈活激勵、吸引及留用激勵對象提供服務，
     motivate, attract, and retain the services of recipients upon whose judgment,      而本公司能否成功經營業務，將主要取決於該

Purpose                                                                         目的
The purpose of the 2019 Share Option Plan is to provide better rewards to       二零一九年購股權計劃旨在向本集團過往及未
the talents who have contributed to the development and success of the          來發展及成功作出貢獻的人才提供更佳回報，
Group, and for the future development of the Group, to motivate such talents    並通過提供購買股份權益的機會，激勵該等人
to continue to make their best efforts and to attract new talents through       才繼續全力以赴及招攬新人才。
providing them with the opportunities to acquire interests in Shares.

     2019 RSU PLAN                                                                     二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃
     The Company has approved and adopted the 2019 RSU Plan by the Board’s             本公司已於二零一九年七月二日（「二零一九年
     resolution on July 2, 2019 (the “Effective Date of the 2019 RSU Plan”).           受限制股份單位計劃生效日期」）以董事會決議
                                                                                       案批准及採納二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃。


     As at the beginning and the end of the Reporting Period, no RSUs were             於報告期初及期末，概無根據二零一九年受限
     granted but yet to be vested under the 2019 RSU Plan. During the Reporting        制股份單位計劃授出但尚未歸屬的受限制股份
     Period, no RSUs were granted, vested, cancelled or lapsed under the 2019          單位。於報告期內，概無根據二零一九年受限
     RSU Plan.                                                                         制股份單位計劃授出、歸屬、註銷或失效的受
                                                                                       限制股份單位。


     Purpose                                                                           目的
     The purpose of the 2019 RSU Plan is to provide better rewards to the talents      二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃旨在向本集團
     who have contributed to the development and success of the Group, and for         過往及未來發展及成功作出貢獻的人才提供更
     the future development of the Group, to motivate such talents to continue         好的回報，通過提供購買股份權益的機會，激
     to make their best efforts and to attract new talents through providing them      勵該等人才繼續竭盡所能及招攬新人才。
     with the opportunities to acquire interests in Shares.


     Eligible participants                                                             合資格參與者

     customer, supplier, service provider, business partner or joint venture partner   (iii) 委員會或管理層（視情況而定）全權酌情認

     be) considers, in its sole discretion, has contributed or will contribute to      諮詢人、顧問、代理、客戶、供應商、服務供
     the Group. The Committee (in respect of Directors and senior management           應商、業務合作夥伴或合營企業合作夥伴。委
     of the Company) or the management (in respect of persons who are not              員會（就本公司董事及高級管理層而言）或管理
     Directors or senior management of the Company) shall have the authority to        層（就非本公司董事或高級管理層之人士而言）

Maximum number of Shares                                                          股份最高數目

under the 2019 RSU Plan was 31,659,128, representing 1.5% of the total            出的受限制股份單位涉及的股份最高數目為
issued share capital of the Company as at August 2, 2019, and, when               31,659,128股，相當於本公司於二零一九年八月

of each share option shall be at least the higher of: (i) the nominal value of   份面值；(ii)於有關購股權授出日期聯交所每日
a Share; (ii) the closing price of a Share as stated on the Stock Exchange’s     報價表所報之股份收市價；及(iii)股份於緊接有
daily quotations sheet on the date of grant of such share option; and (iii)      關購股權授出日期前五(5)個營業日聯交所每日
the average closing price of a Share as stated on the Stock Exchange’s daily     報價表所報之平均收市價。

2022 RSU PLAN                                                                                  二零二二年受限制股份單位計劃
The Company has approved and adopted the 2022 RSU Plan by the Board’s                          本公司已以董事會決議案批准及採納二零二二
resolution taking effective from March 22, 2022 (the “Effective Date of the                    年受限制股份單位計劃，自二零二二年三月二
2022 RSU Plan”). During the Reporting Period, no RSU has been granted by                       十二日起生效（「二零二二年受限制股份單位計
the Company pursuant to the 2022 RSU Plan.                                                     劃生效日期」）。於報告期內，本公司概無根據
                                                                                               二零二二年受限制股份單位計劃授出受限制股
                                                                                               份單位。


Purpose                                                                                        目的
The purpose of the 2022 RSU Plan is to provide better rewards to the talents                   二零二二年受限制股份單位計劃旨在向本集團
who have contributed to the development and success of the Group, and for                      過往及未來發展及成功作出貢獻的人才提供更
the future development of the Group, to motivate such talents to continue                      好的回報，通過提供獲得股份權益的機會，激
to make their best efforts and to attract new talents through providing them                   勵該等人才繼續竭盡所能及招攬新人才。
with the opportunities to acquire interests in Shares.

     2024 SHARE SCHEME                                                                 二零二四年股份計劃
     The Company has adopted the 2024 Share Scheme by an ordinary resolution           本公司已於二零二四年六月二十六日以股東正
     duly passed by the Shareholders on June 26, 2024. As at the Latest Practicable    式通過的普通決議案採納二零二四年股份計
     Date, save for the grant made on June 27, 2024 to Mr. Ma Heping, no share         劃。於最後實際可行日期，除於二零二四年六
     options or awards were granted, vested, exercised, lapsed or cancelled under      月二十七日授予馬和平先生外，概無根據二零
     the 2024 Share Scheme.                                                            二四年股份計劃授出、歸屬、行使、失效或註
                                                                                       銷購股權或獎勵。


     Purpose                                                                           目的
     The purpose of the 2024 Share Scheme is (a) to provide the Company                二零二四年股份計劃旨在(a)向本公司提供靈活
     with flexible means of remunerating, incentivizing, retaining, rewarding,         方式，以向合資格參與者（定義見下文）提供薪
     compensating and/or providing benefits to Eligible Participants (as defined       酬、激勵、挽留、獎勵、補償及 ╱ 或利益；(b)
     below); (b) to align the interests of Eligible Participants with those of the     通過向合資格參與者提供購買本公司股權的機

     COMPLIANCE WITH THE CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                        遵守企業管治守則
     CODE
     The Company had applied the principles and code provisions as set out in        本公司已應用企業管治守則所載的原則及守則
     the Corporate Governance Code and has complied with the code provisions         條文，並於報告期內一直遵守企業管治守則的
     in the Corporate Governance Code for the Reporting Period, except for the       守則條文，惟下文所闡釋偏離守則條文第C.2.1
     deviation from code provision C.2.1 as explained below.                         條的情況除外。


     Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 in Part 2 of the Corporate Governance Code,    根據企業管治守則第2部守則條文第C.2.1條，董
     the roles of chairman and chief executive officer should be separate and        事長與首席執行官的角色應有區分，且不應由
     should not be performed by the same individual.                                 同一人擔任。


     Mr. Ma Heping has been appointed as the Co-Chairman of the Board with           馬和平先生已獲委任為董事會聯席董事長，自
     effect from May 21, 2026. In addition, Mr. Ma Heping also serves as the Chief   二零二六年五月二十一日起生效。此外，馬
     Executive Officer, and is responsible for the implementation of our business    和平先生亦擔任首席執行官，負責我們業務戰
     strategies and the day-to-day business operation. Although Mr. Ma Heping is     略的實施及日常業務營運。儘管馬和平先生同
     both a Co-Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive Officer, the power      時擔任董事會聯席董事長及首席執行官，但董
     of chairman has been shared by the other Co-Chairman, Mr. Jiang Hao, who        事長的權力已由另一名聯席董事長江浩先生分
     would also exercise his power and authorities as a Co-Chairman in managing      擔，江浩先生亦行使其作為聯席董事長的權力
     the affairs of the Board and the Company. Besides, the balance of power         及權責，以管理董事會及本公司的事務。此
     and authority is adequately ensured by the operations of the Board, which       外，董事會由具備充足獨立意見的獨立非執行
     consists of independent non-executive Directors with sufficient independent     董事組成，其運作可充分確保權力與權責的平
     views. Therefore, the Board considers that the balance of power and             衡。因此，董事會認為當前安排下權力及權責
     authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and this structure   的平衡將不會受到損害，且該架構將能使本公
     will enable the Company to make and implement decisions promptly and            司迅速並有效地作出及實施決策。
     effectively.


     The Board will continue to review and consider splitting the roles of           董事會將在考慮本集團的整體情況後，繼續檢
     Co-Chairman of the Board and the Chief Executive Officer if and when it is      討及於適當時候考慮分開董事會聯席董事長與

     INTRODUCTION                                                                       引言
     We have reviewed the interim financial information set out on pages 92 to          本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審閱列載於第92
     166, which comprises the interim condensed consolidated statement of               頁至第 166 頁的中期財務資料，此中期財務資
     financial position of Tongcheng Travel Holdings Limited (the “Company”)            料包括同程旅行控股有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公
     and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) as at 30 June 2026 and the            司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）於二零
     interim condensed consolidated income statement, the interim condensed             二六年六月三十日的中期簡明綜合財務狀況表
     consolidated statement of comprehensive income, the interim condensed              與截至該日止六個月期間的中期簡明綜合收益
     consolidated statement of changes in equity and the interim condensed              表、中期簡明綜合全面收益表、中期簡明綜合
     consolidated statement of cash flows for the six-month period then ended,          權益變動表和中期簡明綜合現金流量表，以及
     and selected explanatory notes. The Rules Governing the Listing of Securities      選定的解釋附註。香港聯合交易所有限公司證
     on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited require the preparation                 券上市規則規定，就中期財務資料擬備的報告
     of a report on interim financial information to be in compliance with the          必須符合以上規則的有關條文以及國際會計準
     relevant provisions thereof and International Accounting Standard 34               則第34號「中期財務報告」。 貴公司董事須負責
     “Interim Financial Reporting”. The directors of the Company are responsible        根據國際會計準則第34 號「中期財務報告」擬備
     for the preparation and presentation of this interim financial information in      及列報該等中期財務資料。我們的責任是根據
     accordance with International Accounting Standard 34 “Interim Financial            我們的審閱對該等中期財務資料作出結論，並

     financial information based on our review and to report our conclusion solely      整體）報告我們的結論，除此之外本報告別無其

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                                   審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with International Standard                 我們已根據國際審閱準則第2410號「由實體的獨
on Review Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial Information               立核數師執行中期財務資料審閱」進行審閱。審
Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity”. A review of interim          閱中期財務資料包括主要向負責財務和會計事
financial information consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons          務的人員作出查詢，及應用分析性和其他審閱
responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying analytical         程序。審閱的範圍遠較根據《國際審計準則》進
and other review procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an      行審計的範圍為小，故不能令我們可保證我們
audit conducted in accordance with International Standards on Auditing            將知悉在審計中可能被發現的所有重大事項。
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we would             因此，我們不會發表審計意見。
become aware of all significant matters that might be identified in an audit.
Accordingly, we do not express an audit opinion.


CONCLUSION                                                                        結論
Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that causes us to          按照我們的審閱，我們並無發現任何事項，令
believe that the interim financial information of the Group is not prepared, in   我們相信    貴集團的中期財務資料未有在各重
all material respects, in accordance with International Accounting Standard       大方面根據國際會計準則第 34 號「中期財務報
34 “Interim Financial Reporting”.                                                 告」擬備。

4. ESTIMATES AND JUDGEMENTS                                                          4. 估計及判斷
  The preparation of the Interim Financial Information requires                       編製中期財務資料要求管理層作出影響會
  management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions that                       計政策的應用及資產及負債、收入及開支
  affect the application of accounting policies and the reported amounts              的呈報金額的判斷、估計及假設。顧名思
  of assets and liabilities, income and expenses. The resulting accounting            義，由此產生的會計估計很少與相關的實
  estimates will, by definition, seldom equal the related actual results.             際結果相等。


  In preparing the Interim Financial Information, the significant                     於編製中期財務資料時，管理層在應用本
  judgements made by management in applying the Group’s accounting                    集團會計政策時作出的重大判斷及估計不
  policies and the key sources of estimation uncertainty were the same as             確定性的主要來源與二零二五年度財務報
  those applied to the 2025 Annual Financial Statements.                              表所採用者相同。

  to valuation techniques used to measure fair value. Such inputs are                 三十日及二零二五年十二月三十一日按公

  •    quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets or           •   相同資產或負債在交投活躍市場的報

                                                                                                （第1層）；


  •    inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1 that are               •   除第1層包括的報價外，該資產或負債
       observable for the asset or liability, either directly (that is, as prices)        的可直接觀察輸入值（即價格本身）或

5. FAIR VALUE ESTIMATION (CONTINUED)                                                    （續）
                                                                                5. 公允價值估計
  (a) Financial instruments in Level 1                                           (a) 第1層金融工具
      The fair value of financial instruments traded in active markets is          於活躍市場買賣的金融工具的公允價
      based on quoted market prices at the balance sheet date. A market            值乃基於結算日市場報價。倘交易
      is regarded as active if quoted prices are readily and regularly             所、交易商、經紀、行業組織、定價
      available from an exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing          服務機構或監管機構隨時定期提供報
      service, or regulatory agency, and those prices represent actual and         價，且該等價格代表實際及定期進行
      regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s length basis.            的公平市場交易，則市場被視為活躍。


  (b) Financial instruments in Level 2                                           (b) 第2層金融工具
      The fair value of financial instruments that are not traded in an            並無於活躍市場買賣的金融工具公允
      active market is determined by using valuation techniques. These             價值以估值技術釐定。該等估值技術
      valuation techniques maximize the use of observable market                   盡量利用既有可觀察市場數據，盡量
      data where it is available and rely as little as possible on entity          少依賴實體的特定估計。倘計算某工
      specific estimates. If all significant inputs required to fair value an      具的公允價值所需的所有重大輸入值
      instrument are observable, the instrument is included in Level 2.            為可觀察數據，則該工具將列入第2
                                                                                   層。


  (c) Financial instruments in Level 3                                           (c) 第3層金融工具
      If one or more of the significant inputs is not based on observable          倘一項或多項重大輸入值並非基於可
      market data, the instrument is included in Level 3.                          觀察市場數據，則該工具計入第3層。


      Specific valuation techniques used to value financial instruments            用於評估金融工具的專門估值技術包
      include:                                                                     括：


      •    Quoted market prices or dealer quotes for similar instruments.          •    市場報價或交易商對類似工具的報
                                                                                        價。


      •    Other techniques, such as discounted cash flow or expected              •    貼現現金流量或預期可收回現金流
           recoverable cash flows analysis are used to determine fair value             量分析等其他技術用於釐定金融工
           for financial instruments.                                                   具公允價值。


      Level 3 instruments of the Group’s financial assets include                  本集團金融資產的第3層工具包括按公
      long-term or short-term investments measured at fair value through           允價值計量且其變動計入損益的長期
      profit or loss and certain derivative financial instruments.                 或短期投資及若干衍生金融工具。

          the Directors of the Company) (the “CODMs”) review and evaluate the          營運決策者」），負責分開審閱及評估該兩
          business performance of these two businesses separately for allocating       項業務的業務表現，以分配資源及作出戰
          resources and making strategic decisions.                                    略決策。


          The CODMs assess the performance of the operating segments                   主要營運決策者主要根據各營運分部的收
          mainly based on revenues and operating profit/loss of each operating         入及經營溢利 ╱ 虧損評估營運分部表現。
          segment. Thus, segment results would present revenue, cost of                因此，分部業績將呈列各分部的收入、銷
          revenue and operating expenses, and operating profit/loss for each           售成本及經營開支以及經營溢利 ╱ 虧損，
          segment, which is in line with CODMs’ performance review. There were         這與主要營運決策者的表現審閱一致。截

          intangible assets as identified from business combinations, certain          開支、業務合併中識別的該等無形資產攤
          corporate administrative expenses and other miscellaneous items) are         銷、若干企業行政開支及其他雜項）並未分

30. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                  30. 關聯方交易
  Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly    倘一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
  or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence       能對另一方的財務及經營決策行使重大影
  over the other party in making financial and operation decisions. Parties      響力，則雙方被視為關聯方。倘雙方受共
  are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control.       同控制，亦被視為關聯方。本集團主要管
  Members of key management and their close family member of the                 理層成員及彼等的近親亦被視為關聯方。
  Group are also considered as related parties.


  Save as disclosed elsewhere in this Interim Financial Information, the         除本中期財務資料其他部分所披露者外，
  following significant transactions were carried out between the Group          以下重大交易乃由本集團及其關聯方於截
  and its related parties during the six months ended June 30, 2026 and          至二零二六年及二零二五年六月三十日止
  2025. In the opinion of the Directors of the Company, the related party        六個月期間進行。本公司董事認為，該等
  transactions were carried out in the normal course of business and at          關聯方交易乃於日常業務過程中按本集團
  terms negotiated between the Group and the respective related parties.         與各自關聯方磋商的條款進行。
